XLPE/SHF1 Cable 
According to IEC 60092-350 
Spec.no. DOC-23-H0001 Rev.5 
Date 2023.06.19 
Page 3 / 64 
 
1. Scope 
This specification covers the minimum requirement of the halogen free cables in accordance with IEC 
60092-350, -352, -353, -354, -360, -376 and relevant standards. 
 
2. Applicable Standard 
IEC 60228        (2004) Conductors of insulated cables   
IEC 60092-350 (2020) Electrical installations in ships. Part 350: Shipboard power cables  
- General construction and test requirements    
IEC 60092-352 (2005) Electrical installations in ships   
Part 352: Choice and installation of cables for low-voltage power systems    
IEC 60092-353 (2016) Electrical installations in ships   
Part 353: Power cables for rated voltages 1 kV and 3 kV   
IEC 60092-360 (2021) Electrical installations in ships  
 Part 360: Insulating and sheathing materials for shipboard and offshore 
units, power, control, instrumentation and telecommunication cables 
  
IEC 60092-354 (2020) Electrical installations in ships  
 Part 354: Single - and three -core power cables with extruded solid 
insulation for rated voltages 6 kV (Um = 7,2 kV) up to 30 kV (Um = 36 kV) 
  
IEC 60092-376 (2017) Electrical installations in ships  
Part 376: Cables for control and instrumentation circuits 150/250V(300 V)  
    
 
IEC 60332-1-2  (2015) Tests on electric and optical fibre cables under fire conditions - Part 1-2: 
Test for vertical flame propagation for a single insulatedwire or cable -  
Procedure for 1 kW pre-mixed flame   
IEC 60332-3-22 (2018) Tests on electric cables under fire conditions -  
Part 3 -22: Test for vertical flame spread of vertically -mounted bunched 
wires or cables - Category A   
IEC 60331-1      (2018) Tests for electric cables under fire conditions - Circuit integrity   
Test method for fire with shock at a temperature of at least    
at 830 °C for cables of rated voltage up to and including 0,6/1,0 kV and  with 
an overall diameter exceeding 20 mm   
IEC 60331-2      (2018) Tests for electric cables under fire conditions - Circuit integrity   
Test method for fire with shock at a temperature of at least 830 °C for 
cables of rated voltage up to and including 0,6/1,0 kV and with an overall 
diameter not exceeding 20 mm   
IEC 60331-21      (1999) Tests for electric cables under fire conditions ;  
Procedures and requirements – Cables of rated voltage up to and including 
0.6/1kV   
IEC 61034-1,2     (2019) Measurement of smoke density of cables burning under defined conditions   
IEC 60754-1,2     (2019) Test on gases evolved during combustion of materials from cables   \n\n--- PAGE 4 ---\n

IEEE 1580          (2021) IEEE Recommended Practice for Marine Cable for Use on Shipboard and 
Fixed or Floating Facilities  
 
 
UL 1581              (2017) Reference Standard for Electrical Wires, Cables, and Flexible Cords 
  
LSGS-11-RC0101 LS cable Specification for XLPE insulated SHF1 sheathed cable 
  
LSGS-16-RC0056 LS cable Specification for XLPE insulated SHF1 sheathed cable

* For VFD Cable, depending on the nominal voltage rating and the reflected voltage, over insulated conductor ratings 
is considered. It should have an overall non-ferrous shield with a coverage of 100%. The purpose of thisoverall shield is to minimize 
EMI emissions. The insulation material used on these cables have a dielectric constant less than 3.0 to reduce cable capacitance.

4. Test items and method 
The following test shall be carried out in accordance with IEC 60092-350,-353,-354,-376 and this spec. 
 
4.1 Routine test 
Clause Test Item Test Method Remark 
4.1.1 Conductor resistance test IEC 60092-350, 5.2.2  
4.1.2 Voltage test IEC 60092-350, 5.2.3  
4.1.3 
Insulation resistance test IEC 60092-350, 6.9 LV cable only 
(Except F-GO cable) 
Screen insulation resistance IEC 60092-376, 17.1 Instrument cable 
only 
 
4.2. Sample test 
Clause Test Item Test Method Remark 
4.2.1 Conductor examination test IEC 60092-350, 6.4  
4.2.2 Check of dimension test IEC 60092-350,6.5~6.7  
4.2.3 Hot set test IEC 60092-350, 6.8  
 
4.3. Type test, Electrical 
Clause Test item Test Method Remark 
4.3.1 Insulation resistance at room temperature IEC 60092-350, 7.2.1 Except F-GO cable 
4.3.2 Insulation resistance at operating temperature IEC 60092-350, 7.2.2 Except F-GO cable 
4.3.3 Increase in a.c capacity after  immersion in water test IEC 60092-350, 7.3  
4.3.4 High voltage test for 4h IEC 60092-350, 7.4 LV cable only 
4.3.5 Mutual capacitance test IEC 60092-350, 7.5 Control & 
Instrument cable 
only 4.3.6 The inductance to resistance ratio IEC 60092-350, 7.6 
 
4.4. Type test, Non-Electrical 
Clause Test item Test Method Remark 
4.4.1 Non-electrical characteristics of insulation IEC 60092-360  
4.4.2 Non-electrical characteristics of sheath IEC 60092-360  
4.4.3 Measurement of thickness of insulation IEC 60092-350, 8.2  
4.4.4 Measurement of thickness of sheath IEC 60092-350, 8.3  
4.4.5 Additional ageing test(compatibility test) IEC 60092-350, 8.6  
4.4.6 Test for the behavior of sheath at high temperature IEC 60092-350, 8.8  
4.4.7 Test for the behavior of sheath at low temperature IEC 60092-350, 8.9  
4.4.8 Test for the metal coating of copper wire IEC 60092-350, 8.11  
4.4.9 Test for resistance of halogen-free SHF1 sheaths to 
cracking (heat shock test) IEC 60092-350, 8.13  
4.4.10 Flammability test IEC 60332-1-2  
4.4.11 Flame retardant test IEC 60332-3-22 Cat. A  \n\n--- PAGE 7 ---\n

Clause Test item Test Method Remark 
4.4.12 Fire resistance test IEC 60331-1 & 2 “FRT-” type only

5. Mechanical & Electrical Properties 
5.1 Insulation (Comply with IEC 60092-360) 
Description Unit XLPE 
Maximum rated conductor temperature  ℃ 90 
A. Without aging    
1. Min. Tensile strength N/㎟ 12.5 
2. Min. Elongation at break  % 200 
B. After aging in air oven without copper conductor (135±3℃/ 168hrs)    
1. Max. variation for Tensile strength              % ±25 
2. Max. variation for Elongation at rupture         % ±25 
C. Hot set test (200℃±3℃/10min., 20N/㎠)    
1. Max. Elongation under load  % 175 
2. Max. Elongation after cooling  % 15 
D. Compatibility test (100℃±2℃ / 168hrs)   
1. The variation of Tensile strength  % 25 
2. The variation of Elongation at rupture % 25 
E. Determination of degree of acidity (IEC 60754-2)   
1. Min. PH  - 4.3 
2. Max. conductivity  ㎲/mm 10 
F. Amount of halogen acid gas:   
1. Max. HCl and HBr (IEC 60754-1) % 0.5 
2. Max. HF (IEC 60684-2:2011) % 0.1 
G. Min. Constant for Insulation resistance    
1. at 20℃ - 3670 
2. at 90℃ - 3.67

5.2 Outer Sheath (Comply with IEC 60092-360)  
 
Description Unit SHF1 
A. Without aging    
1. Min. Tensile strength     N/㎟ 9 
2. Min. Elongation at break  % 120 
B. After aging in air oven (100℃±2 /168hr)    
1. Min. Tensile strength 
-  Max. variation for Tensile strength              
N/mm2 
% 
7.0 
±30 
2. Min. Elongation at break 
- Max. variation for Elongation at rupture         
% 
% 
110 
±30 
C. Pressure test at high temperature    
1. Max. permissible deformation (80℃±2/ 4hr : dia. ≤12.5mm) % 50 
2. Max. permissible deformation (80℃±2/ 6hr : dia. >12.5mm) % 50 
D. Heat shock test (150 ℃±3/1hr) - No crack 
E. Behavior at low temperature   
1. Cold bending test (-15℃±2 /16hr) : dia. ≤12.5mm - No crack 
2. Min. Elongation (-15℃ ±2 /4hr) : dia. > 12.5mm % 30 
3. Cold impact test (-15℃ ±2 /16hr) - No crack 
F. Determination of degree of acidity (IEC 60754-2)   
1. Min. PH  - 4.3 
2. Max. conductivity  ㎲/mm 10 
G. Amount of halogen acid gas   
1. Max. HCl and HBr (IEC 60754-1) % 0.5 
2. Max. HF (IEC 60684-2:2011) % 0.1 
H. After oil immersion (ASTM No.2/ IRM 902, 70 ℃±1/4hr)   
1. Max. variation for Tensile strength % ±40 
2. Max. variation for Elongation at rupture % ±40 
 
6. Cable marking 
The following information shall be printed on the surface of the cable through the length of cable  
at least 1m interval. 
 
- Voltage rating 
- Cable type 
- Numbers  of core(s), pair(s) or triad(s) and Conductor size 
- Manufacturer’s name 
- Year of Manufacture 
- Application standard (if necessary) 
- Length (in every meter) \n\n--- PAGE 9 ---\n 
 
XLPE/SHF1 Cable 
According to IEC 60092-350 
Spec.no. DOC-23-H0001 Rev.5 
Date 2023.06.19 
Page 9 / 64 
 
 
* Example. 
0.6/1kV F-TCOTBO 3C X 4SQMM LS CABLE [YEAR] IEC 60332-3A 0001M  
 
* Note. Cable identification & Marking according to customer requirement can be applied primarily. 
 
7. Drum Marking 
The metallic labels reporting the  following information shall be attached on the flange surface of the 
wooden drum. 
 
- Project name  
- Voltage rating/ Cable type 
- Numbers of core(s), pair(s) or triad(s) 
- Conductor size 
- Manufacturer’s name 
- Length 
- Drum length 
- Net & Gross weight 
- End point  
- Arrow of Rolling direction 
 
8. Packing 
Each end of each cable shall be hermetically sealed with a cap, or other suitable means, to protect against 
the ingress of moisture. Cable shall be packed so completely as not to be damaged during transportation 
or storage. 
 
9. Construction and Material 
The construction and material of cable shall be given in the following table. 
 
* Note: Where these cables are terminated within designated hazardous areas in ships or offshore units, 
special gas barrier glands are to be used to achieve the appropriate seal, such that gas can not migrate 
through the cables as per 3.16 and 4.6 of IEC 60092-350.
